## [3] correct=True · `tpi-non-disclosure-agreement_1.txt`

- request_id: `9fb5c2ec45e4`
- error_type: `none`
- latency_ms: 42146.86

### Query

Consider tpi's Non-Disclosure Agreement; Does the document indicate that the Agreement does not grant the Receiving Party any rights to the Confidential Information?

### Gold

5. No rights or obligations other than those expressed and recited herein are to be implied from this Agreement. In particular, no licenses are hereby granted directly or indirectly under any patent, copyright, or trademark now held by or which may be obtained by, or which is licensable by Disclosing Party, including but not limited to, any license to make, use or sell any product embodying any Proprietary Information.
Disclosing Party hereto confers no right to Receiving Party to use in advertising, publicity, or otherwise any trademark or trade name of Disclosing Party, nor confers any authorization to Receiving Party to act as an agent on its behalf for any purpose.

### RAG Answer

Yes. The TPI Non-Disclosure Agreement indicates that it does not grant the Receiving Party rights to the Confidential/Proprietary Information beyond those expressly stated.

Specifically:

- **Section 5** states that **“No rights or obligations other than those expressed and recited herein are to be implied from this Agreement.”**
- It further states that **no licenses are granted directly or indirectly** under any patent, copyright, or trademark held or obtainable by the Disclosing Party, including **no license to make, use, or sell any product embodying Proprietary Information**.
- It also says the Disclosing Party **confers no right** to the Receiving Party to use its trademarks, trade names, or act as its agent.
- **Section 2** limits the Receiving Party’s use of Proprietary Information to **the purposes described in the Agreement** and requires strict confidentiality.
- **Section 4** confirms that nothing in the Agreement requires disclosure of Proprietary Information and requires return or destruction upon request.

So, the Agreement does **not grant the Receiving Party any general or IP-related rights** to the Confidential/Proprietary Information; it only permits limited use for the Agreement’s stated purposes under confidentiality obligations.

### Judge

- reason: 系统回答覆盖了金标的关键要点，即 NDA 未暗示或授予接收方对机密/专有信息的权利，尤其是不授予知识产权许可及商标/代理权利；额外扩写内容不影响命中。
- gap: 无

(5) is independently developed by an employee of the Receiving Party who has not had access to the information disclosed hereunder.

### RAG Answer

Yes — but only in a limited, conditional sense.

TPI’s Non-Disclosure Agreement defines “Proprietary Information” broadly, but it expressly excludes information that is “independently developed by an employee of the Receiving Party who has not had access to the information disclosed hereunder.”

Therefore, the Receiving Party may independently develop information that is similar to the Confidential/Proprietary Information, provided that:

3. the development was not based on, derived from, or used the disclosed Confidential Information.

However, the Agreement also states that no licenses or rights are granted under patents, copyrights, trademarks, or other intellectual property, and it does not authorize the Receiving Party to use Proprietary Information except for the purposes described in the Agreement. So independent development does not give the Receiving Party the right to use or rely on TPI’s Confidential Information itself.

### Query

Consider the Non-Disclosure Agreement between CEII and NDA; Does the document require the Receiving Party to notify the Disclosing Party if they are required by law, regulation, or judicial process to disclose any Confidential Information?

### Gold

Except as allowed under Section 5, Required Disclosure, the Receiving Party shall not submit CEII obtained from the Disclosing Parties to any government agency for any reason without first obtaining written permission from the respective Disclosing Party, and then fully complying with the requirements of 18 C.F.R. §§ 388.112, 113 for requesting special treatment of the CEII.
In the event that the Receiving Party is requested or required by depositions, interrogatories, requests for information or documents, subpoena, civil investigation, demand or similar process (i) to disclose any CEII or other Confidential Information received pursuant to this Agreement, (ii) to disclose any discussions pertaining thereto, or (iii) to take any other action described in the last paragraph of Section 3 above, the Receiving Party shall provide to the relevant Disclosing Parties prompt written notice of such request(s) and shall use reasonable efforts to resist disclosure until an appropriate protective order may be sought. If, in the absence of a protective order, Receiving Party is nonetheless, in the written opinion of its counsel, legally required to disclose CEII or other Confidential Information received pursuant to this Agreement, then, in such event Receiving Party may disclose such information after the Receiving Party gives the Disclosing Party written notice of the proposed disclosure and a reasonable opportunity to review the proposed disclosure.

### RAG Answer

Yes. Under Section 5, “Required Disclosure,” the Receiving Party must provide prompt written notice to the relevant Disclosing Parties if it is requested or required by depositions, interrogatories, requests for information or documents, subpoena, civil investigation, demand, or similar process to disclose Confidential Information or CEII. It must also use reasonable efforts to resist disclosure until a protective order may be sought. If, after counsel’s written opinion, disclosure is legally required despite the absence of a protective order, the Receiving Party may disclose only after giving written notice of the proposed disclosure and a reasonable opportunity for the Disclosing Party to review it.

### Judge

- reason: 系统回答覆盖了金标关于被要求披露保密信息时须及时书面通知披露方、合理努力抵制披露，以及在法律强制披露情况下须先通知并允许审阅的关键要点，与金标一致。

### Query

Consider VELCO's Non-Disclosure Agreement; Does the document indicate that the Agreement does not grant the Receiving Party any rights to the Confidential Information?

### Gold

If the Recipient is an employee of a federal or state agency, he/she must note that the Classified Information is not the property of the agency, and is not subject to Freedom of Information Act, Vermont’s Public Records Act , or similar statutes.

### RAG Answer

Based on the provided excerpts, the Agreement does **not contain an explicit clause** stating that it grants the Receiving Party “no rights” to the Confidential Information. However, it strongly indicates that the Receiving Party has **no ownership or unrestricted rights**, only a limited permission to use the information.

For example, the document says that CEII and BCSI information is provided **“on loan,”** that the information is **not the property** of the Recipient or the Recipient’s agency, that the Recipient must use the Classified Information **solely for the specified work**, and that the Recipient must **return or destroy** the information if VELCO requests.

So, the document implies that the Agreement does **not grant the Receiving Party any broad rights** to the Confidential Information, but it does not state that in those exact terms.

### Judge

- reason: 系统回答覆盖了金标的关键要点，即涉密信息不是机构财产且接收方不享有所有权或不受限制权利；虽未逐字提及 FOIA/公共记录法，但从宽判定已命中核心结论。

## [237] correct=True · `VELCO%20NDA%20rev0%20Dec%2014%202015.txt`

- request_id: `dd644f11db6f`
- error_type: `none`
- latency_ms: 42654.58

### Query

Consider VELCO's Non-Disclosure Agreement; Does the document require that all Confidential Information be expressly identified by the Disclosing Party?

### Gold

For purposes of this agreement, CI shall mean any confidential, proprietary or trade secret information that is owned or controlled, or is specifically marked or identified as “Confidential”, by VELCO prior to disclosure to Recipient.

### RAG Answer

No—not uniformly.

Under the VELCO NDA, “Classified Information” includes CEII, BCSI, and business Confidential Information (CI):

- For **CI**, the document defines it as information “owned or controlled” by VELCO **or** “specifically marked or identified as ‘Confidential’” by VELCO prior to disclosure. So express marking/identification is not always required for CI.
- For **CEII** and **BCSI**, the document requires that VELCO **designate** the information as such, and written CEII/BCSI must be labeled “Confidential CEII” or “Confidential BCSI.”

So the document does **not** require all Confidential Information to be expressly identified by the Disclosing Party; it imposes express designation/labeling requirements mainly for CEII and BCSI, while CI may be covered if owned/controlled by VELCO even if not expressly marked.

### Judge

- reason: 系统回答准确覆盖金标关键要点，即 CI 可基于 VELCO 拥有或控制而成立，不一定需要明确标记；其额外关于 CEII/BCSI 的说明不影响对金标的命中。
- gap: 无

### Query

Consider VELCO's Non-Disclosure Agreement; Does the document allow verbally conveyed information to be considered as Confidential Information?

For purposes of this Agreement, “BCSI” shall mean: (i) all information designated as such by VELCO, whether furnished before or after the date hereof, whether oral, written or recorded/electronic, and regardless of the manner in which it is furnished; and
For purposes of this Agreement, CEII shall mean: (i) all information designated as such by VELCO, whether furnished before or after the date hereof, whether oral, written or recorded/electronic, and regardless of the manner in which it is furnished; and
CI subject to this Agreement may be in intangible form, such as information communicated orally or by visual observation, or may be embodied in tangible form, such as a document.

### RAG Answer

- 对于 Business Confidential Information（CI），协议明确说明 CI 可以以“intangible form”存在，例如“information communicated orally or by visual observation”。
- 对于 BCSI 和 CEII，协议也规定信息无论以 oral、written 或 recorded/electronic 形式提供，只要由 VELCO 指定为 BCSI 或 CEII，即受保护。

### Judge

- reason: 系统回答覆盖了金标的关键结论，即口头信息可属于协议保护的信息，并准确提及 BCSI/CEII 和 CI 的口头形式要点，未与金标矛盾。

### Query

Consider VELCO's Non-Disclosure Agreement; Does the document allow the Receiving Party to share some Confidential Information with third parties, including consultants, agents, and professional advisors?

### Gold

The foregoing notwithstanding, the Recipient may disclose classified information to its employees or contractors (hereafter Representatives) to the extent each such Representative has a need to know such information to conduct the work referenced in the Whereas clauses, and shall comply with Recipient’s obligations under this Agreement.

### RAG Answer

No—not broadly. VELCO’s NDA generally prohibits the Receiving Party from divulging Classified/Confidential Information to any third party without VELCO’s prior written consent.

It does allow limited disclosure to the Receiving Party’s **employees or contractors** (“Representatives”) if each has a need to know the information for the work described in the NDA and must comply with the NDA’s obligations. The document does **not expressly permit sharing with agents or professional advisors**. Consultants are not listed in the main permitted-disclosure clause, although the company NDA signature page requires the company to assume responsibility for employees’, contractors’, and consultants’ compliance.

### Judge

- reason: 系统回答已经覆盖金标的关键要点，即允许向员工或承包商在need-to-know和遵守义务条件下披露机密信息；其对agents/professional advisors的额外限定不改变对金标要点的命中。
- gap: 无

### Query

Consider VELCO's Non-Disclosure Agreement; Does the document require the Receiving Party to notify the Disclosing Party if they are required by law, regulation, or judicial process to disclose any Confidential Information?

### Gold

5. In the event that the Recipient is required to disclose Classified Information by subpoena, law or other directive of a court, administrative agency, or arbitration panel, the Recipient will provide VELCO with immediate notice of such request in order to enable VELCO (and Recipient shall cooperate fully with VELCO) to seek an appropriate protective order or other remedy.

### RAG Answer

Yes. Under Section IV, paragraph 5 of the VELCO Non-Disclosure Agreement, if the Recipient is required to disclose Classified Information by subpoena, law, or other directive of a court, administrative agency, or arbitration panel, the Recipient must provide VELCO with immediate notice of such request. The Recipient must also cooperate with VELCO to seek a protective order or other remedy and consult with VELCO to identify steps to quash or narrow the scope of the request.

### Judge

- reason: 系统回答覆盖了金标的关键要点：法律/监管/司法要求披露时的立即通知义务，以及配合保护令/救济义务；虽措辞和引用细节略有扩展，但不影响命中。
